# OfficeIMO.Operations operation contract

Schema version: 1

Each row retains its detailed owning catalog and evidence. A supported row may still carry a bounded limitation.

| Package | Format | Target | Operation | State | Capability | Source contract | Public API | Evidence | Boundary |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `OfficeIMO.Adf` | ADF | HTML | Convert | Supported | adf-html | OfficeConversionCapabilityCatalog | `AdfConverter.ToHtml(AdfDocument.Parse(json), htmlOptions, options)` | Typed ADF fixtures verify recursive structure, strong/emphasis/strike/underline/subscript/superscript marks, links, tables, extension preservation, HTML projection, and deterministic fidelity diagnostics. | ADF text/background colors and arbitrary HTML styling are retained only when the intermediate Markdown profile can represent them; unsupported nodes and marks use explicit diagnostics or extension preservation. |
| `OfficeIMO.Adf` | ADF | Markdown | Convert | Supported | adf-markdown | OfficeConversionCapabilityCatalog | `AdfConverter.ToMarkdown(AdfDocument.Parse(json), options)` | Typed ADF fixtures verify recursive structure, strong/emphasis/strike/underline/subscript/superscript marks, links, tables, extension preservation, HTML projection, and deterministic fidelity diagnostics. | ADF text/background colors and arbitrary HTML styling are retained only when the intermediate Markdown profile can represent them; unsupported nodes and marks use explicit diagnostics or extension preservation. |
| `OfficeIMO.Adf` | HTML | ADF | Convert | Supported | html-adf | OfficeConversionCapabilityCatalog | `AdfConverter.FromHtml(html, options)` | Typed ADF fixtures verify recursive structure, strong/emphasis/strike/underline/subscript/superscript marks, links, tables, extension preservation, HTML projection, and deterministic fidelity diagnostics. | ADF text/background colors and arbitrary HTML styling are retained only when the intermediate Markdown profile can represent them; unsupported nodes and marks use explicit diagnostics or extension preservation. |
| `OfficeIMO.Adf` | Markdown | ADF | Convert | Supported | markdown-adf | OfficeConversionCapabilityCatalog | `AdfConverter.FromMarkdown(markdown)` | Typed ADF fixtures verify recursive structure, strong/emphasis/strike/underline/subscript/superscript marks, links, tables, extension preservation, HTML projection, and deterministic fidelity diagnostics. | ADF text/background colors and arbitrary HTML styling are retained only when the intermediate Markdown profile can represent them; unsupported nodes and marks use explicit diagnostics or extension preservation. |
| `OfficeIMO.AsciiDoc.Markdown` | AsciiDoc | Markdown | Convert | Partial | asciidoc-markdown | OfficeConversionCapabilityCatalog | `AsciiDocDocument.ParseResult(source).Document.ToMarkdownDocumentResult(options)` | Typed fixtures verify the documented bounded AsciiDoc subset, deterministic output, and explicit diagnostics. | Includes, extensions, macros, complex attributes, custom processors, and unsupported inline or block syntax are not a full AsciiDoc implementation. |
| `OfficeIMO.AsciiDoc.Markdown` | Markdown | AsciiDoc | Convert | Partial | markdown-asciidoc | OfficeConversionCapabilityCatalog | `MarkdownDoc.Parse(markdown).ToAsciiDocDocumentResult(options)` | Typed fixtures verify the documented bounded AsciiDoc subset, deterministic output, and explicit diagnostics. | Includes, extensions, macros, complex attributes, custom processors, and unsupported inline or block syntax are not a full AsciiDoc implementation. |
| `OfficeIMO.AsciiDoc.Pdf` | AsciiDoc | PDF | Convert | Partial | asciidoc-pdf | OfficeConversionCapabilityCatalog | `AsciiDocDocument.ParseResult(source).Document.ToPdfDocumentResult(options)` | Typed fixtures verify the documented bounded AsciiDoc subset, deterministic output, and explicit diagnostics. | Includes, extensions, macros, complex attributes, custom processors, and unsupported inline or block syntax are not a full AsciiDoc implementation. |
| `OfficeIMO.Confluence` | Confluence | HTML | Convert | Partial | confluence-html | OfficeConversionCapabilityCatalog | `ConfluenceContentConverter.ToHtml(page)` | Materialized Confluence page and body fixtures exercise ADF and storage representations through the shared ADF, Markdown, and HTML converters with combined diagnostics. | This is content-body conversion, not live page synchronization; Confluence-only macros, layouts, extensions, and presentation outside the supported ADF/storage subset may be simplified. |
| `OfficeIMO.Confluence` | Confluence | Markdown | Convert | Partial | confluence-markdown | OfficeConversionCapabilityCatalog | `ConfluenceContentConverter.ToMarkdown(page)` | Materialized Confluence page and body fixtures exercise ADF and storage representations through the shared ADF, Markdown, and HTML converters with combined diagnostics. | This is content-body conversion, not live page synchronization; Confluence-only macros, layouts, extensions, and presentation outside the supported ADF/storage subset may be simplified. |

| `OfficeIMO.Confluence` | Markdown | Confluence | Convert | Partial | markdown-confluence | OfficeConversionCapabilityCatalog | `ConfluenceContentConverter.FromMarkdown(markdown, format)` | Materialized Confluence page and body fixtures exercise ADF and storage representations through the shared ADF, Markdown, and HTML converters with combined diagnostics. | This is content-body conversion, not live page synchronization; Confluence-only macros, layouts, extensions, and presentation outside the supported ADF/storage subset may be simplified. |

| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Supported | PowerPoint.Ppt.AccessibilityMetadata | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.AccessibilityMetadata::modern-to-legacy | OfficeArt object names and descriptions decode and project to native non-visual metadata for supported slide, master, layout, group, connector, picture, media, OLE, and converted static-visual shapes. Fresh PPT/POT/PPS writing and preservation-aware imported edits add, replace, or remove the native Unicode name and description properties while retaining unrelated OfficeArt properties. Modern accessibility titles and decorative state have no classic binary representation and are explicitly loss-blocked. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Rejected | PowerPoint.Ppt.Actions | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.Actions::modern-to-legacy | InteractiveInfoAtom click and mouse-over records decode to typed action metadata. Built-in slide-show jumps, macro names, program targets, named custom shows with show-and-return, animated-highlight state, stop-sound state, and referenced embedded sounds project and write natively, including preservation-aware add/edit/remove. Legacy OLE or media activation and visited-state behavior remain preserve-only; Open XML actions without an exact classic action are explicitly loss-blocked. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Rejected | PowerPoint.Ppt.ActiveX | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.ActiveX::modern-to-legacy | ExControlContainer metadata, ExObjRefAtom shape references, optional icon metafile, and compressed or uncompressed ExControlStg records decode to a typed neutral model with exact Office Forms storage bytes. No-op saves and unrelated supported binary edits retain the source records and storage exactly. Control authoring, property editing, and Open XML projection remain loss-blocked until the binary control state can be mapped without changing behavior. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Rejected | PowerPoint.Ppt.Animations | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.Animations::modern-to-legacy | The complete 28-byte AnimationInfoAtom decodes to the typed binary model, including flags, sound reference, delay, order, slide count, build type, classic effect and direction, after-effect, text subdivision, OLE verb, raw dim color, and reserved trailing bytes. Supported top-level shape animations project to editable PresentationML timing, write natively to PPT/POT/PPS, and can be added, replaced, or removed through preservation-aware incremental records with shared embedded sounds. Recognizable PPTX entrance animEffect/bldP sequences—including PowerPoint's visibility-set scaffold—convert to the exact classic subset. With-previous concurrency, exit/emphasis effects, motion paths, arbitrary property/color/scale/rotation/set/command behavior, media timing, custom after-effect trees, and other modern timing semantics are explicitly loss-blocked; inline sound overrides and imported group-child animation edits remain preservation-guarded until their owning media and group paths are implemented. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Rejected | PowerPoint.Ppt.AutoShapes | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.AutoShapes::modern-to-legacy | OfficeArt preset geometry with a DrawingML equivalent is projected as an editable native shape, including arrows, callouts, flowcharts, ribbons, stars, and action buttons. Fresh PPT/POT/PPS writing maps the canonical classic preset families back to native OfficeArt shape types. All eight signed adjustment slots are decoded without losing their shape-specific meaning; exact round-rectangle and donut adjustments project, author, and edit natively. Unmodified imported geometry is byte-preserved, and supported transform or style edits retain it. Other edited preset adjustments, custom geometry, text warps, and DrawingML-only presets are explicitly loss-blocked to prevent silent geometry changes. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Rejected | PowerPoint.Ppt.Backgrounds | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.Backgrounds::modern-to-legacy | OfficeArt background shapes are distinguished from normal drawing content. Solid, no-fill, inherited, multi-stop linear/path gradient, texture, and picture backgrounds project to native Open XML fills on slides and all master types. Fresh PPT/POT/PPS writing emits explicit solid, no-fill, multi-stop linear, scaled, centered-path, and shape-path gradients plus stretched embedded raster or metafile picture backgrounds on slides, notes pages, main masters, notes masters, and handout masters. Picture backgrounds share the document BLIP catalog, deduplicate identical payloads, and materialize layout backgrounds onto each affected slide. The classic foreground/background opacity ramp preserves linearly interpolated gradient-stop opacity. Supported imported slide, notes-page, main-, title-, notes-, and handout-master background edits, including stretched picture additions, replacements, and removals, append preservation-aware records. Picture edits retain the original document and Pictures streams as exact prefixes, balance existing BLIP reference counts, and append deduplicated delayed BLIPs; ordinary PPTX-layout background edits materialize into every affected imported slide. Gradient focus, custom path rectangles, non-linear stop opacity, pattern fills, tiled or cropped pictures, picture effects, and exact texture semantics without a safe classic mapping are explicitly loss-blocked during conversion. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Supported | PowerPoint.Ppt.BuiltInProperties | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.BuiltInProperties::modern-to-legacy | SummaryInformation and DocumentSummaryInformation project title, subject, creator, keywords, description, category, revision, edit/print/create/modified times, application, presentation format, slide/note/hidden counts, manager, and company to the normal package and application-property surfaces. Fresh PPT/POT/PPS writing emits Unicode property sets. Supported imported edits replace only the owning compound stream; unrelated binary edits retain unknown properties byte-for-byte, while a metadata edit that would discard an unknown property id or type is loss-blocked. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Supported | PowerPoint.Ppt.BulletsAndNumbering | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.BulletsAndNumbering::modern-to-legacy | Base character bullets project and write native enabled/disabled state, glyph, resolved font and color, relative or point size, level, and ruler indentation. PPT9 StyleTextProp9Atom and TextMasterStyle9Atom extensions decode and author all 41 classic automatic-numbering schemes with exact start values. PPT9 picture-bullet collections and references import and author with exact image bytes. Fresh files and append-only imported shape or supported main-master edits support bullet or numbering add, change, and removal while preserving unrelated programmable-tag data. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Partial | PowerPoint.Ppt.Charts | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.Charts::modern-to-legacy | Supported editable DrawingML charts convert explicitly to deterministic static PNG picture frames through the shared OfficeIMO chart renderer. Default saving is blocked by PPT-WRITE-CHART-CONVERTED until PowerPointSaveOptions.LossPolicy is Allow; converted files preserve chart bounds and visible data but not chart editability or the embedded workbook. Existing legacy Microsoft Graph objects remain preserve-only through the embedded-OLE lane; editable Graph import and direct native Graph authoring are explicitly outside the conversion contract and structural edits are loss-blocked. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Partial | PowerPoint.Ppt.ColorSchemes | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.ColorSchemes::modern-to-legacy | All eight classic scheme colors decode and map consistently between binary background/text/shadow/title/fill/accent slots and Open XML Light1/Dark1/Accent4/Dark2/Light2/Accent1/Accent2/Accent3. Fresh PPT/POT/PPS writing emits a distinct native classic scheme for every referenced master plus slide and notes-page color overrides, and no-op imported saves preserve original scheme records. The DrawingML round-trip theme extension preserves PPTX Accent5, Accent6, hyperlink, and followed-hyperlink colors exactly while retaining the classic eight-color fallback. Imported main-, title-, notes-, handout-master, and existing slide or notes-page theme color edits append synchronized native classic and DrawingML scheme records; ordinary layout color overrides materialize into affected slides. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Supported | PowerPoint.Ppt.Comments | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.Comments::modern-to-legacy | PowerPoint 2000-2003 PP10 comments decode with author, initials, text, index, UTC creation time, and master-unit position. Classic comments project editably, write natively to PPT/POT/PPS, and imported additions, edits, and removals append preservation-aware slide records without replacing unrelated PP10 tag data. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Rejected | PowerPoint.Ppt.Connectors | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.Connectors::modern-to-legacy | Straight, bent, and curved OfficeArt connectors are projected as native editable connection shapes. Fresh unattached connectors write native OfficeArt geometry, transforms, line styling, and arrowheads. Solver rules preserve imported start/end shape attachments and connection-site indexes, while supported top-level transform and style edits append preservation-aware records. Fresh or edited attachment rules are explicitly loss-blocked because emitting a different binary solver graph could silently change connectivity. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Supported | PowerPoint.Ppt.CustomProperties | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.CustomProperties::modern-to-legacy | UserDefinedProperties dictionaries and text, Boolean, date/time, floating-point, signed/unsigned integer, and binary values project to the native Open XML custom-properties part and write to fresh PPT/POT/PPS files. Supported imported add/edit/remove operations rewrite only DocumentSummaryInformation; unrecognized property sections or variants remain byte-preserved for unrelated edits and loss-block metadata changes. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Supported | PowerPoint.Ppt.CustomShows | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.CustomShows::modern-to-legacy | NamedShows records decode to typed ordered slide-id sequences and project to native custom-show lists. Fresh PPT/POT/PPS writing and imported add/edit/remove preserve custom-show membership and identity incrementally. Custom-show actions, including show-and-return, project and round-trip natively. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Rejected | PowerPoint.Ppt.CustomXml | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.CustomXml::modern-to-legacy | PowerPoint 97-2003 has no equivalent package-part representation. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Rejected | PowerPoint.Ppt.DigitalSignatures | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.DigitalSignatures::modern-to-legacy | The root _signatures stream and complete _xmlsignatures storage are detected and reported. Exact no-op saves preserve the signed package byte-for-byte. Edited binary saves block by default; callers can explicitly retain the invalidated carrier for preservation-aware edits or remove both carriers. Creating signatures and translating Open XML signatures into the binary formats are outside the writer contract. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Supported | PowerPoint.Ppt.EmbeddedOle | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.EmbeddedOle::modern-to-legacy | ExOleEmbedContainer metadata, ExObjRefAtom shape references, and compressed or uncompressed ExOleObjStg records decode to an editable PowerPointOleObject with exact compound-storage bytes. Fresh PPT/POT/PPS writing emits native embedded-object metadata, storage, shape references, and embedded raster or metafile preview images through the shared BLIP catalog, including supported crop and picture effects. Imported geometry, ProgID, icon/content view, color-follow, and storage replacement use preservation-aware incremental records while untouched metadata, preview pictures, compound streams, and compressed source records remain exact. Malformed storage, unsupported related parts, linked previews, and non-representable preview metadata are loss-blocked rather than silently discarded. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Rejected | PowerPoint.Ppt.EmbeddedVideo | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.EmbeddedVideo::modern-to-legacy | PowerPoint 97-2003 has path-based AVI and MCI movie objects but no native embedded-video payload representation; implicit extraction to an external path is deliberately loss-blocked. |
| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Supported | PowerPoint.Ppt.Encryption | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.Encryption::modern-to-legacy | Password-protected PPT/POT/PPS files decode and encode through the normal detached encrypted-load/save APIs. The Current User token, canonical single UserEdit and PersistDirectory records, CryptSession10Container, per-persist-object keys, and field-scoped Pictures transforms follow the binary PowerPoint encryption contract. SHA-1 RC4 CryptoAPI keys from 40 through 128 bits are supported. EncryptedSummary document properties are restored on import and encrypted by default on save; callers can explicitly retain clear-text properties for compatibility. RC4 CryptoAPI is provided only for legacy interoperability and is not a modern security choice. Cross-generation conversion reports that password protection is removed and blocks by default; callers must explicitly select a loss policy and re-encrypt the modern output when confidentiality must continue. |

| `OfficeIMO.PowerPoint` | PowerPoint | PowerPoint.Ppt | Convert | Supported | PowerPoint.Ppt.Groups | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt | `PowerPointCompatibilityCatalog.Current` | OfficeIMO.PowerPoint.LegacyPpt::PowerPoint.Ppt.Groups::modern-to-legacy | Nested OfficeArt group hierarchies and child coordinate systems project as native editable Open XML groups. Fresh PPT/POT/PPS writing emits real nested group containers, group coordinate records, child anchors, and recursively cataloged pictures and interactions. Picture backgrounds share the document BLIP catalog, deduplicate identical payloads, and materialize layout backgrounds onto each affected slide. The classic foreground/background opacity ramp preserves linearly interpolated gradient-stop opacity. Supported imported slide, notes-page, main-, title-, notes-, and handout-master background edits, including stretched picture additions, replacements, and removals, append preservation-aware records. Picture edits retain the original document and Pictures streams as exact prefixes, balance existing BLIP reference counts, and append deduplicated delayed BLIPs; ordinary PPTX-layout background edits materialize into every affected imported slide. Gradient focus, custom path rectangles, non-linear stop opacity, pattern fills, tiled or cropped pictures, picture effects, and exact texture semantics without a safe classic mapping are explicitly loss-blocked during conversion. |

Unsupported master objects, rich shape styling, interactions, transitions, timing, and unprojected later-version style fields are loss-blocked. Fresh authoring allocates slide-master, notes-master, slide, notes-page, and handout persist and drawing identifiers dynamically up to the binary format's persist-directory and OfficeArt limits. DocumentAtom notes and handout master references, schemes, shapes, placeholders, and connector rules project into native notesMaster and handoutMaster parts. |
